# Commit log

## 1. `328b7d3` Port Timbre to native Swift and SwiftUI for iOS (#1)

*2026-10-22T19:33:25-03:00 · azerish25-ux*

* build(ios): establish Swift project and macOS verification workflow

* feat(ios): port musical core, catalogue, vocal scoring and durable history to Swift

* fix(ios): simplify Swift vocal contour type inference

* feat(ios): connect native SwiftUI app, audio, StoreKit and simulator checks

* feat(ios): preserve output audio and ad policy, add full integration coverage

* fix(ios): close source parity gaps in history, tuning cues and lifecycle

* fix(ios): resolve SDK and drawing compilation, localize measured voice results

* build(ios): verify App Store SDK and paid simulator delivery flows

* fix(ci): select installed Xcode 26.3 and valid release context

* fix(ios): prevent unconfigured ad SDK launch crash and close review findings

* test(ios): isolate simulator verification and export visual evidence

* fix(ios): handle real permission and keyboard flows with atomic selections

* fix(ios): stabilize StoreKit refreshes and finish native practice controls

* fix(ios): preserve paused pitch and verify tablet layouts

* fix(ios): declare elapsed timer privacy reason

* test(ios): capture settled iPad landscape display

* fix(ios): prioritize completed sessions in recent instruments

* test(ios): confirm tablet tab destination after rotation

* test(ios): synchronize verified purchases and permission dismissal

* docs(ios): record verified port and Apple delivery handoff

---------

## 2. `be17ba8` Add procedural 3D instrument pipeline and CI result publishing

*2026-10-22T20:33:35-03:00 · Claude*

Blender (headless, CI) builds parametric guitar, bass and ukulele models
from code, exports USDZ with rig anchors named after catalogue anchor ids,
and renders studio previews. Results publish to ci-results/* branches.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
Claude-Session: https://claude.ai/code/session_019ufcyLVTAsS1wDMvGhgM3C


## 75. `43b1849` DSP: chord recognizer and onset detector for chord and rhythm lessons

*2026-10-23T04:05:01-03:00 · Claude*

ChordRecognizer turns 4096-8192 sample frames into a chord reading:
spectral peaks above the band's noise floor, a tuning offset from the
peaks, a semitone spectrum with each string's overtones removed from low
to high, a compressed 12-bin chroma smoothed across frames, and cosine
matching against major, minor, 7, maj7, m7, sus2, sus4 and power-chord
templates on all 12 roots with a small bass-root preference. matches()
answers lesson checks with tolerance for inversions and voicings.

OnsetDetector is streaming SuperFlux-style spectral flux with an
adaptive threshold, one frame of look-ahead and a refractory interval,
so strums and ringing notes report once. RhythmJudge pairs beats with
onsets one to one (perfect <= 40 ms, good <= 90 ms) and scores accuracy,
counting extra strums inside the exercise.

Tests synthesise plucked-string chords (open, barre, power, every
root/quality from ChordFinder, harder conditions), noise and silence,
click tracks, ringing notes, sixteenths and a strum lesson end to end.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
Claude-Session: https://claude.ai/code/session_019ufcyLVTAsS1wDMvGhgM3C

## 94. `35fc427` Polish pass from review round 2

*2026-10-23T07:35:12-03:00 · Claude*

- Paywall: plans visible above the fold with a compact hero, a save-% tag against monthly, a pinned bar that nothing hides under; the trial reminder is only promised when notifications can deliver it
- Tuner: one colour rule tied to the lock band, digits and status from the same rounded value, redundant turn pill removed; 3D stage marks the peg to turn with direction; blank stage race fixed
- Practice tools: edge-to-edge chip rails that keep the selection in view, metronome and drone docks, chord play buttons and finger numbers, scale labels clear of dots
- Progress and Play: aligned achievements with progress rings, string-health bars, quest counts; ear training rebuilt with round dots, combo, sounds and a results card
- Onboarding: no pre-selected answers, placement from experience, a Your plan screen before the paywall; lessons get visuals on every explain step, Hear it on chords, landing fingers, strum arrows
- Streak freezes in the streak chip and toast; app job timeout 45 min

## 105. `356daa3` Polish pass 3 from review round 3

*2026-10-23T09:58:21-03:00 · Claude*

- Tuner: steady readout (10 Hz, hysteresis, fixed-width ¢ column), precision setting (±5/±3/±1¢, Pro for ±1¢ and the strobe band), lock bell at the string's pitch with automatic detection paused while it rings, chip label readable through the lock, camera dolly instead of a cross-fade, string drawn along its real anchors
- Rewards: a Dynamic-Island pill that queues one banner at a time instead of covering the header; new plucked/bell sound palette
- Paywall: plans, trial timeline and CTA fit a 6.1-inch screen; headline follows the reason (locked lesson card, instrument, drone…)
- Onboarding: a first-tune moment before the plan; Get started; legible disabled Continue
- Lessons: tap-to-pass only without a mic (Practice mode, max one star), neck strip on note steps, strum sweep, strings thicker toward the bass, zoom into lessons, graded score colour, bar counter fixed
- Practice: curated guitar chord shapes with fingers and tests, intervals on scales with a scrolling neck that follows playback, one tuning quest counter everywhere, docks that never hide content, top scrims, no truncation
- App Store listing localized for 9 locales

*2026-10-23T17:08:06-03:00 · Claude*

- Placement pass: the first lesson of the placed unit is free, so the plan and the lesson that opens agree (AccessPolicy, tested)
- Instrument voices: Karplus–Strong plucks with strum spread and body resonance, bowed/wind/sung voices with vibrato; Hear it, chord play buttons, listen steps and reference tones (stable pitch) per instrument; singing exercises use a sung note
- Play-along: rosewood runway, glossy gems with sustain tails, hit beams and bursts, combo multiplier, a next-notes rail; strum view on a wooden soundboard; lanes follow the new String order setting
- Paywall: outcome bullets for the user's goal, a live mini play-along preview, their first milestones above the trial track
- Live Activity and Dynamic Island for metronome, drone and practice sessions
- Plural rules (stringsdict) for all 48 locales

## 262. `b2af55a` Design system, app shell and rewards: docked reward toast, colour roles, a real Calm mode, one streak lamp

*2026-10-25T13:37:03-03:00 · Claude*

The reward toast no longer imitates the Dynamic Island: island mode is deleted and every
announcement drops in as a capsule of the app's glass at safeAreaInsets.top + 8, at the
leading gutter on a phone and centred on wide screens, so nothing is drawn inside the
status bar. Placement keeps both cases for callers; RootView mounts it docked.

Colour roles: Palette.adjust names the status amber for non-pitch uses (restring soon,
a coach's fix, a moved peg, an early or late grade) and every owned file asks for a role
instead of Palette.amber or Palette.coral (zero direct uses outside Theme.swift). Today's
activities are categories in accentNeutral (the lesson in learning violet), drawn as bare
glyphs rather than tinted discs; rewards (Today complete, bursts, the XP tile, best take)
use starGold; the results accuracy tile is neutral and the burst behind the stars drops
its outer ring so it never crosses the headline. controlLine rises to 0x646A74 (3.23:1 on
surface). Every off-scale Typeface literal in owned files moves to the type scale (19).
Dock backdrops are opaque stage below a 24 pt fade.

Calm mode now hides the header's streak chip and level ring, XP, level, quest and streak
toasts, the results' XP and streak tiles and level bar (accuracy and minutes instead),
Today's bonus and streak count, Learn's XP suffix, quest XP, Progress's streak and level
tiles (this week's minutes instead), the week card's streak and friends' standing, the
coach's streak nudge, the posters' streak and the milestone celebration. The Settings
footer says so.

One streak metaphor: TimbreGlyph.lamp, a hanging stage lamp with its beam, replaces the
flame on the chip, tiles, coach, Today, posters and streak badges (the flame stays for the
play-along combo); the unused StreakFlame view is removed. Results draw the level as the
header's ring around "LV n". After a clean take the coach gives the next lesson instead of
restating the headline. The first three-star song asks for a rating
(ReviewPrompt.afterSong) and peaks offer ReferralPill.

Settings: Tune by feel leads the Tuning section; the Pro row reads "Every lesson and song,
every tuning and tool". Instruments: the picker opens on its search with tunings after the
shelf, TuningMenu gives the tuner header a tunings menu, "Try next" moves into the caption
and Learn's PRO pills become the corner lock. Brand: TimbreMark and TimbreLogotype replace
tracked amber capitals on the song poster. Medals draw their studio-lit artwork from the
Medals folder once it ships; Brand and Medals namespace folders are in the catalog.

App icon: an interim mint "Still Point" with real Default, Dark (transparent backdrop) and
Tinted (grayscale) appearances, readable at 40 px, until the designer's Icon Composer file
lands. #Previews for the design components live in Timbre/Previews (debug only).

## 263. `4d826e2` Tuner: a lock that holds on screen, Tune by feel on the tuner, whole heads in every close-up

*2026-10-25T13:37:53-03:00 · Claude*

The lock
- TuningTracker keeps a confirmed lock across a gap of up to 500 ms when the readings either side are in the band;
  a hold still building never counts a gap, and the band and the 650 ms hold are unchanged. TuningReading.status is
  a Codable TuningStatus enum (string spellings still read as the cases while callers migrate).
- LockLatch holds the lock on screen (badge, quiet status line, solid chip, peg tick, aura, VoiceOver) for at least
  1.5 s and then while the pitch stays in the band; it ends on a pitch outside the band for 0.2 s, another target,
  silence after the minimum, a restarted hold, pausing or leaving the tuner.
- The lock path in AppStore.accept saves the string sample, the lock's progress and the full-tune count through
  updateSettingsSoon; the reward is announced once the progress is saved.

Tune by feel
- A Feel toggle (hand.tap) among the tuner's side controls; on by default only while VoiceOver runs until the player
  chooses (TuneByFeel); a one-time TipKit tip after the third full tune. The side column packs tighter, then drops
  captions, on short stages.
- Each beat is scheduled as one CHHapticParameterCurve on the engine clock instead of per-frame parameters.

Stage
- The 3D close-ups keep the head's crown (every mesh vertex above the top peg, measured once per model) under the
  feathered top edge instead of one tip point, so violin and cello scrolls and the mandolin's head stay whole; phone
  and iPad share one vertical fit. Studio cards take the wood above the pegs from their rig.
- Stages pin left-to-right; the close-up leans away from the side controls on either side (and for a mirrored stage),
  peg labels keep clear of them and set their words in the host's direction. A debug-only probe reports the head's
  clearance.

Readout and type
- Hertz formatted for the locale ("82,41 Hz"), the note letter cross-fades, Auto/Manual/Reference in sentence case at
  15 pt, "≈ estimate" at 12 pt, off-scale Typeface sizes in Tune/ moved onto the scale, amber kept to status.

## 266. `2319909` Feature-scoped tuner state, background persistence and test hooks out of release paths

*2026-10-25T13:39:26-03:00 · Claude*

Tuner state moves out of AppStore into an @Observable TunerSession (Services/TunerSession.swift): the string or
chromatic note being tuned, automatic detection, the strings locked this session, the celebration tick, a game's or
lesson's target and the session's measurements. AppStore forwards its existing API (selectedCourse, referenceMode,
automatic, lockedCourses, gameTargetHz, ...), so every screen compiles unchanged, but an automatic string change, a
chromatic note change, a lock or a lesson's next target no longer fires AppStore.objectWillChange: only views that
read those values redraw. The store is also injected as an environment Observable for views that move to it.

The lock path saves nothing on the frame that locks. The string locks at once; its progress event, the full-tune
count (with the review request) and the string-health sample are handed to the next updateSettingsSoon batch
(about 300 ms), applied in order, and flushSettings applies them early.

LocalStore gains a durability mode. Background durability (used by AppStore) changes the state at once and writes the
file on a serial utility queue: saves made while a write runs collapse into one write of the newest state, a failed
write is reported and retried by the next save or flush, and flush() waits for the disk. Immediate durability keeps
the previous write-before-return semantics for tools and tests. Saves validate only what they change (the settings, or
the appended record; the whole history after a roll-up) instead of re-validating every record. The writer and the
state file are Sendable through Synchronization.Mutex, with no unchecked conformances.

Dead settings are removed (theme, skin, reducedMotion, telemetryEnabled, bandOnSpeaker); the lenient decoder skips
their keys in older files. UserDefaults keys used by the app's own code go through one Preferences type
("learn.course", "plan.dailyMinutes", "analytics.proActive"). TestSupport gains typed launch keys and storageRoot;
AppStore, Bootstrap, GameCenter, LiveActivityController, SpotlightIndex and YourSongsStore read it instead of the
process environment, so none of them reads the environment in a release build. Debug demo data moves to Support.

Launch decodes the catalogue off the main thread beside the curriculum, and a curriculum that fails to decode logs
curriculum_failed. NoteNames.active and WatchBridge's foreground flag are Mutex-backed for Swift 6. Widget, Live
Activity, Siri snippet and watch type sizes move onto the type scale (hero numerals use Typeface.hero), and status
colours there and on the launch notices give way to flame, starGold, accentNeutral or inkSoft.

Tests: TunerSession unit tests, an AppStore test that a string change publishes nothing on the store while its
readers are told, a lock test that rewards arrive with the next batch, background-durability tests (coalescing,
failure and retry) and a seeded fuzz test that a state file cut off or torn at any byte still opens.

## 268. `f824a90` Play-along reads fingers on fingerboards, songs play and judge every chord change

*2026-10-25T13:42:35-03:00 · Claude*

Fingerboards (violin, viola, cello, double bass): a new FingerPlace reads a
note's place as its players do (finger, 0 open, low/high of its first-position
place, or a shifted position). The runway's gems, the up-next rail, the Next row
and VoiceOver say "String 1, finger 1" instead of a semitone fret; spoken cues
stop naming frets there; FretboardDiagram puts a cello's tapes on its own
first-position places and reads its dots by finger.

Chords per change: SongArrangement.played(inBar:) gives each bar's chords as
played (the downbeat's, then every change inside the bar). LeadChart.filledBars
turns them into ChordBar values; SongPlayback builds the band from them through
BandArrangement.Bar.segments (song performance, play-along steps, the song
sheet's preview and loops). ChordAlongJudge now judges chords at arbitrary beats
(every downbeat and every mid-bar change, each on its own first stroke) and
scores sections by beat range. "Chords & words" shows split bars ("C · G") with
the beat dots split where the chord changes, and names each change ahead of it.

Lanes and RTL: string 6 moves to ice (0xB8F0FF) and every gem carries its
string number; the up-next rail, take ribbon, timing strip, section ribbon,
song map and chord lane stay left to right in RTL languages; the Next row
cross-fades instead of rolling letters as digits; Esc drops a lesson take;
spoken cues start two beats ahead under a 1.5 s beat and go brief under a
1.6 s gap, through SpokenCues.schedule. Combo heat and stars use gold, the
song map's hook gold and orchid instead of amber and coral.

Results: section tiles keep a minimum width, fall back to "In"/"Out" and never
go blank; "Strongest" skips sections under eight notes and breaks ties by note
count; a song that stays above 90 % says "Every section 90%+".

Show me: one stable "Chart · Player view · 3D" picker (ChordPictureToggle with
an optional 3D binding) replaces the relabelling toggle; the button's disc says
"3D" in letters; ShowMe.lastProblem exists in debug builds only. Lesson clips
show a photo stepper (<name>, <name>-2, ...) until a video is bundled.

Your songs: ChartTake plays a pasted chart start to finish as a "Chords &
words" take (SongChordsTake now takes a ChordTakeSheet, so a lesson and a chart
share it), offered from the practice summary.

Off-scale Typeface sizes in these files moved onto the type scale. Tests cover
mid-bar chord judging and that every song's played bars reproduce its chart's
changes.

## 270. `94da7e9` Audio: a speaker kit band, chord changes inside a bar, ambient UI sounds, timed spoken cues

*2026-10-25T13:45:42-03:00 · Claude*

The band now reaches players on the phone's speaker, follows every chord change, and the
app stops interrupting the user's audio.

Band (Timbre/Audio/Band.swift, TimbreCore BandGroove):
- BandTrack renders three layers: the kit (drums and cymbals, plus the crash and choke
  one-shots), the pitched rhythm section (bass line and toms) and the comping. On the
  speaker while the microphone listens the band plays its kit only, high-passed at 200 Hz
  and a little softer, so nothing pitched reaches the pitch detector. Thin and fill stay
  audible there: a thinned kit steps back and returns at four in a row. With nothing
  listening, or in headphones, the full band plays.
- AudioService holds the band to its kit by route (BandPlayer.routeKitOnly) and mirrors it
  in BandFeel.kitOnly; playBand gains onSpeaker (default true) so an attack-graded step can
  keep the speaker to the click.
- Bar.segments/changes are rendered: BandGroove.changing(at:) cuts bass notes and chord
  strokes that ring across a change and strikes the new chord on it; each note and stroke
  takes the chord of the segment it starts in, the pad falls back per segment, and the
  final chord is the one the last bar ends on. Tests cover the split in every style.

Session (AudioService.swift, RecordingPlayer.swift, SoundFX.swift, PlayAlongCues.swift):
- AudioSessionOwner runs every session change on its serial queue, never holds its lock
  across a session call, waits a one-second grace before deactivating (an activation calls
  it off), leaves the session ambient afterwards, and gives the microphone path an
  off-main activation. SoundFX and Earcons ask it for the ambient category before
  preparing or playing, so the welcome motif mixes with a podcast and chimes keep to the
  silent switch once the tuner has stopped.
- A route change and the engine's configuration change share one debounced rebuild, which
  restarts capture only if the engine stopped or the tapped input changed.
- Interruptions for a suspended app are ignored (SessionInterruption, shared with the
  recording player); a restart that comes while the app is inactive resumes when it is
  active again.
- 20 ms I/O buffers when the microphone only listens (the tuner), 5 ms when timing
  matters. Readings reach the main actor through one coalesced hop, and the pitch worker
  emits a reading per hop however the input is chunked.
- RecordingPlayer's observers are removed by a Sendable bag, not a main-actor deinit.

Spoken cues (CueVoice.swift, PlayAlongCues.swift):
- SpokenCues.schedule speaks through the app's own AVSpeechSynthesizer in the app's
  language, choosing the full phrase, the brief one ("6 open") or none so the cue ends
  before its note; it learns its own timing from each utterance. SpeechGate sets the
  microphone's readings and frames aside while the voice speaks. Note names come from
  NoteNames.spokenInApp. Voice processing stays off.
- Pitchless swishes mark tier-ups and broken runs of x3 and more on the speaker.

docs/ios/AUDIO-DEVICE-TESTS.md: the device test sheet (results pending a device run).

## 272. `0d0f4b0` Paywall and first session tell the truth: a free first song, a Free/Pro table built from the policy, working price levers

*2026-10-25T13:46:22-03:00 · Claude*

First song
- The plan's days follow the minutes a day the person chose (PaywallPlan.Pace), not one lesson a day.
- For the "songs" goal, when the course's own first song is Pro or more than a week off, the plan names a song the
  free plan plays: the first free song ahead in the course, else the instrument's songbook song on day 2. Onboarding's
  "Play your first song this week" is said only for such a song, so it holds on the free plan too.

Free/Pro table and levers
- PlanComparison (TimbreCore) builds the table from AccessPolicy, FreeTier and YourSongsPolicy: open chords free,
  barre chords/scales/intonation/drone Pro, one custom tuning against unlimited, every tuning free on guitar, bass,
  ukulele, violin and cello, strobe and temperaments Pro, ten charts. FreeTier.Tool names the tool locks.
- timbre_pro_annual_b/_c join TimbreProduct.all, .subscriptions and .yearly: a remote arm sells them and they grant Pro.
- FreeOpenings carries freeSongs and flags.unit2Free into AccessPolicy.canPlay (RemoteConfig applies them as it loads).
- Any plan's free trial is disclosed and handled: trialDays/trialLabel(for:), the month's "7 days free", its CTA,
  terms, trial=1 and the trial reminder. The exit offer's intro card no longer poses as a special offer.
- Subscription outcomes logged on device: subscription_state, renewal, refund.

Paywall seams
- The plan tag is part of its card; the chosen plan's glass is a layer behind the card, so the cards' words never
  wait on it. Cards carry role identifiers (paywall.plan.yearly…).
- One TrackLayout for days, nodes and words: words stay on their node, close days never collide, no amber.
- "12 × $6.99" instead of a struck-through price; "Cancel anytime in Settings"; the rating from the App Store's
  lookup for the buyer's storefront (StoreRating), in ink.

Onboarding
- "Just tune" on the welcome: the tuner listening after one tap, the questions deferred to Learn
  (OnboardingDeferral, OnboardingView(entry: .questions)).
- Restore as a footnote link; the progress bar sized for the longest path; the reminder card after the first chord;
  a Tune by feel offer with a three-second feel after the first lock; the played chord shown on the plan and recorded
  against its lesson; an opaque dock; the chip row keeps Violin and the chosen instrument visible.
- Off-scale type and status colours migrated in the owned files; automation checks read TestSupport.isAutomated.

Tests: the table against the policy, remote openings, yearly arms granting Pro (SKTestSession), per-plan trials,
a beginner's free first song on guitar, ukulele, violin and mandolin, the minutes pace; UI tests for the plan cards on
the Settings route and "Just tune" reaching a listening tuner.

## 274. `face00c` Grade muted, accented and ninth-chord strokes correctly; speak the next note in the notes step; feed recordings through the live analysis path

*2026-10-25T13:47:49-03:00 · Claude*

Strummed takes are now judged stroke by stroke in TimbreCore (StrumTake), which the rhythm step drives:
- Muted beats (uke chunks, mandolin and reggae chops) pass when no chord rings or the stroke has stopped
  150 ms on (ring under 30 % of the attack); a chop left ringing is held to OK and called "Let it stop".
  Muted strokes no longer count toward chordsMostlyWrong, so a correct man-4-4 take earns three stars and
  a correct uke-8-1 take scores 100 % (it scored 60 % before).
- Accented beats must come in 4 dB over the median of the plain strokes; a soft one is held to OK and the
  badge names the beat to push ("Push the and of 2").
- The beat lane marks muted strokes with a cross and accents with an accent mark; upcoming up strokes are
  drawn in steel rather than the status amber.

StrokeChords reports each stroke's attack and ring, and reads a stroke only up to just before the next one
when that comes within the frame (never under 85 ms), so close strokes no longer blend.

ChordTarget checks every quality the curriculum and chord charts name: a 9 as its 7 (E9 as E7), add9 and m6
as their triads, 6 as the minor seventh it equals; diminished, diminished seventh and augmented have
templates of their own, naming a reading only when none of the eight common ones fits.

LessonListening (TimbreCore) is the analysis LessonAnalyzer runs on the audio queue, and AudioClip hands a
recording over in the microphone tap's 512-frame buffers, so tests and the real-world corpus drive
recordings through the same path as a live take. expected.json gains mute and accent clips and budgets.

VoiceOver in the notes step names the next note after each hit and, on the tuner's cadence, which way to go
while a wrong or out-of-tune pitch is held. CuePlan schedules play-along cues two beats ahead, brief when
notes come a beat apart, never overlapping each other or the note they name.

Lesson keys answer page-turner pedals in every mode (Page Down, down and right arrows, Return, space for
primary; Page Up, up and left for again); Slower moves to Option-down. A listening question answered wrong
shows its notes on a staff; after two wrong answers in a lesson, questions show their notes up front and
offer "I can't hear this", which leaves the question unscored.

## 277. `99a553c` App Preview gates, event-anchored lock sync, store-frame crops and a keyword plan App Store Connect accepts

*2026-10-25T13:54:50-03:00 · Claude*

The App Preview pipeline only checked mean loudness, so a cut that was 77 % silent, with a click track under "Play
songs with the band" and the poster before the lock, printed "Meets the App Preview spec". The render now measures
what it delivers and fails when a cut can't ship.

tools/marketing/gates.py (new): sound measured on preview.mp4 (no silence over 0.5 s anywhere, every clip sounding,
the band's low end under a "band" caption); the readout's mint share frame by frame to find the In tune badge's
first frame; a sync screenshot found again to 1/60 s.

preview.py: clips can be anchored on one sync (the lock, taken the moment the app notes it); the lock's bell is
checked against the badge's first frame (at most 80 ms; --reanchor for a reviewed re-cut); the poster is verified
on the badge or moved to the first frame that shows it; the app's band events, the test's x4 check and its missed
moments feed PREVIEW.txt; the app's motif plays under the end card; the duration note reports the delivered length;
cut.json and a `check` command re-measure a rendered cut. Exit 4 marks a take worth recording again.
soundtrack.py reads extra streams and exposes the app's moments, cues and band state.

DemoScenes.swift: preview-tune notes each lock within 10 ms and voices the demo string in the instrument's own
reference voice, following every reading, so the first seconds have the string drifting into tune; the recorder
logs band start/stop with the route, engine onsets and plays the motif on request.

AppPreviewUITests: lock sync on the app's event, readout region, poster at lock + 0.3 s, lock clip to lock + 1.2 s;
the highway from the store frame's clean take (bar 15) with a x4 check; results from the first star; Songs with the
song of the day's preview playing; the motif for the end card.

TimbreUITests / TabletScreenshotsUITests: store frame #1 (iPhone and iPad) requires the In tune badge as well as the
tick; new captures for the violin results and the cello and ukulele pages' lead frames.

screenshots.py: #7 and #8 crops start in gaps and render refuses any frame whose top edge cuts through text; #3's
callout enlarges the ticked strings; --check enforces Apple's Custom Product Page rule (page keywords come from the
keyword field, one page each), the translators' variables against the build, the translators' source against the
English listing, current TRANSLATE cells and per-locale page keywords.

Docs: keyword field rebuilt as the pages' inventory; Custom Product Page and deep-link wording matched to Apple's
help; cello and ukulele page sets; violin page #3 from violin results; "hears every strum" and Tune by feel in the
listing; Accessibility Nutrition Labels; no monthly introductory offer until the paywall discloses it.
preview.yml records both cuts by default, retakes on exit 3 or 4 and publishes the violin cut separately.

## 278. `4fe4432` Release gate, localization gate and CI: a 15-minute required gate, nightly captures, strict App Store translation gate

*2026-10-25T13:55:12-03:00 · Claude*

CI
- ios.yml is the pull-request gate. The required `gate` check passes only when core (engine tests with a TimbreCore
  coverage floor, resources, translation handoff, critical-string gate, comment hygiene, SwiftLint against a committed
  baseline), app (TimbreAppTests plus SmokeUITests: fresh launch, a string locked, Pro bought) and a Release device build
  are green; the three run side by side. `accessibility` runs the audit and the localization launches beside them.
- Compiler warnings fail core, app and release through tools/ci/build_warnings.py; concurrency diagnostics are counted
  per file instead, since complete checking is now on as warnings (SWIFT_STRICT_CONCURRENCY=complete in project.yml,
  StrictConcurrency in Package.swift) and the compiler can't promote every other warning while leaving those alone.
- Pull requests may not carry commits that touch ios/ and skip CI or are WIP snapshots (skip_ci_guard.py; history before
  the day-1 contracts is grandfathered). No path filter, so the required check always reports.
- Write access is limited to one small publish job per workflow; the other jobs upload their results as artifacts.
- nightly.yml (nightly, dispatch, or the `captures` label): the full UI tour and App Store captures (still published to
  ci-results/app), the launch-time metric, the unsigned archive, the iPad shelf, an accessibility matrix (AX5, Bold
  Text, Increase Contrast, left-handed, upside down, Arabic) and the thread sanitizer.

Translation gate
- critical.py checks the shipped locales from CFBundleLocalizations; pull requests fail on a legal, permission or
  VoiceOver gap that tools/l10n/critical-baseline.json doesn't list (--write-baseline records one deliberately).
- apple-delivery.yml: the untranslated escape hatch is now `internal_testflight_untranslated`, which exports with
  testFlightInternalTestingOnly (not submittable for review or external testing) and stamps TimbreBuildChannel=internal.
  `ship_min_coverage` applies a language bar to one upload.
- tools/l10n/shipping.py reports coverage per locale and, when the owner chooses, trims CFBundleLocalizations and the
  build's .lproj folders to locales at or above a bar. Every locale still ships; nothing was cut.
- report.py lists launch-critical strings first (legal, permission, VoiceOver, onboarding funnel) and --strict fails CI on
  an out-of-date handoff; HANDOFF.md and the TODO files are regenerated.

Tests
- test10PlayAlongLesson: the note steps were starved, not stuck. Each waitForExistence poll snapshots the accessibility
  tree and stalls the app's main thread, where the demo pitch, the note hold and the step hand-off run; a passing run
  already spent 13 s of the 25 s budget, a busy runner ran past it. The test now leaves the explanation by tapping until
  it is gone, skips the tune check only when it shows, keeps its hands off while the notes play and polls sparingly.
- CommerceIntegrationTests: purchases go through buy(), which retries a purchase that failed before StoreKit recorded
  anything and refreshes entitlements while waiting (30 s), with the session's state in the failure message.
- AccessibilityAuditUITests enforces contrast, hit regions, element descriptions and traits (known issues listed per
  screen), retries an audit that times out and fails if none completes, launches with TIMBRE_STAGE_REST, and adds the
  lesson steps, the play-along before Start and the metronome.
- LocalizationLaunchUITests: the German headline check (an expected failure until the funnel strings are translated), an
  Arabic check that the octave sits right of the note read from the screenshot, and a bounded-pseudolanguage check that no
  text on the welcome screen or the tuner bypasses the string tables.

Lint
- lint_tells.py is now comment_hygiene.py (lint_tells.py forwards to it); tool names are no longer policed, review-panel
  references are; new status-colour and inline-orb rules; a warn rule arms itself once its baseline count reaches 0.
- Bundle.app names the app-language bundle for lookups that pass one explicitly.

## 282. `c46c452` Songbooks and first songs: free unit-1 songs, corrected ukulele keys, cello and bass songbooks, Songs you know shelf

*2026-10-25T13:56:43-03:00 · Claude*

Curriculum
- Every instrument course's unit 1 now ends on a free, arranged song: Mary Had a
  Little Lamb in three notes on guitar, mandolin, violin, cello, bass, banjo and
  voice; Row, Row, Row Your Boat on one chord for ukulele (uke-2-1 moved ahead so
  the unit still ends on the song). Tunes and words live in tunes.py with their
  public-domain credits.
- editions.py: the songbook key comes from the opening and closing chords (the
  tonic, with the opening chord's mode), chord roots are spelled for the key
  (Eb, never D#), and ukulele keys pay for hard shapes (Cm, Fm, Eb, Bb7, F#m,
  Db), so Rondo alla Turca sits in A minor and Carmen in A. Octave folds move
  whole phrases of three or more notes and never turn a step into a leap.
- New cello songbook (CGDA shapes, first position, 65 songs) and bass songbook
  (roots and fifths on the beat, 120 songs, no lyrics); both registered in
  build.py.
- Muted and accented beats emitted for reggae skanks, mandolin chops, ukulele
  chunks and island accents through the lib.py muted/accents parameters.
- Public-domain clearance doc lists the first songs and the December carols.
- Regenerated curriculum.json and the English Learn.strings.

App
- SongOfTheDay: songsYouKnow (Ode to Joy, Amazing Grace, Silent Night, ...),
  carols and carol season; a known tune leads the pick until three are played;
  Curriculum.distinctTunes counts each tune once across versions.
- SongsView: "Songs you know" shelf first, a Carols shelf in season ("free this
  week" when RemoteConfig.freeSongs opens them), a gold Pro lock on Pro sleeves,
  and a "Request a song" footer that logs song_request.
- SongCover: the pulse motif is replaced by a tuner dial motif.
- Chords: maj9, m9, 6/9, 7sus4, 11, 13, dim7 and m7b5 formulas; PitchSniper keeps
  bowed instruments' hidden tunes in first position.
- Practice views: status colours kept for status (gold for goals and roots,
  steel for tools), on-scale type sizes, localized counts.

Tests: songbook edition tests (keys, spelling, folds, ranges), first-song and
songbook-size checks, distinct tune counting, hidden-tune coverage.

## 286. `2b78d1e` Tuner, settings, onboarding, commerce and Learn shell take the wave-19 shared pieces

*2026-10-25T14:42:54-03:00 · Claude*

TuningStatus cases replace status strings in AppStore.statusText, LiveReading, FirstTuneStep and TunerSessionTests. Settings binds Tune by feel through TuneByFeel (binding and isOn for the feel test), lists only languages with a .lproj in the bundle, and gates strobe and temperaments through FreeTier.opens. The tuner header's tuning line is the TuningMenu (tunings, new custom tuning, instrument picker) while the instrument name still opens the picker. Share cards and the welcome carry TimbreLogotype; streak chips draw the lamp glyph; the tune celebration leaves XP and streak out in Calm mode. Learn asks the questions Just tune deferred. learn.course reads go through Preferences; environment reads through TestSupport. A verified purchase that hasn't shown up in the entitlements yet is checked again a second later. canPlay passes the remote settings' free openings explicitly. PRIVACY.md covers the song_request event; APPLE-DELIVERY.md covers the annual_b/annual_c products, the show-only rating key, freeSongs and the delivery workflow's translation-gate inputs.

## 288. `8580079` Platform integration: resting stage for audits, neutral Today arcs on the watch, tune count by tune, capture waits

*2026-10-25T14:45:28-03:00 · Claude*

TestSupport gains TIMBRE_STAGE_REST (`stageResting`, read once): StagePower, the while-seen modifier in Kit and
ShaderFX treat it as Low Power Mode, so the accessibility audits read a screen that holds still. RootView,
StrumModel and InstrumentStage read their launch keys through TestSupport instead of the process environment
(CoachVoice and TimbreTips already did).

WatchBridge sends the Today arcs as the iPhone draws them: "neutral" (accentNeutral, 0xB4BECB) for the tools and
the review, "violet" for the lesson, never a status colour. WatchTheme adds accentNeutral, orchid and lime and maps
names as Theme.swift does (the curriculum's "amber"/"coral" courses onto orchid/lime). The watch app's icon is the
iPhone's current one. The Live Activity's downbeat dot and accented beats are white, as the metronome's meter
shows an accent, instead of the status amber.

Curriculum.distinctTuneCount counts `distinctTunes` (one song however many lessons, instruments or treatments;
exercises excluded).

RootView's debug probe dates the latest reward handed to the toast (`reward=`). UI tests: the tuner captures 07,
14, 17 and 18 and the tune-locked frames (17b and the others) wait the reward toast out; the tune-locked frames
check the lock stays on screen for 1.5 s (stage.state lock=1); the mandolin, violin and cello stages check the head
clears the stage's top edge once the close-up is framed (stage.head); test01 meets the first chord before the
reminder card, the order onboarding now uses.

## 291. `e7dcdfd` Lessons, songs and play-along integration: one cue path, fingers on fingerboards, a truthful band chip

*2026-10-25T14:50:43-03:00 · Claude*

- Chord cards (chord step, chord explanation) carry one "Chart · Player view · 3D" switch wherever a model exists;
  the strum and neck rows show ShowMeToggle with the model or without. ShowMeCardHeader removed (unused).
- Violin and cello never say "fret": the notes step words and VoiceOver use NotePlace.words, and every
  FretboardDiagram and ShowMe.words in the lesson steps get the course fingerboard. The finished chord title is ink.
- Attack-graded takes (rhythm steps, Chords & words) play the band with onSpeaker: false.
- Spoken cues: play-alongs and the final take plan with CuePlan (SpokenCues.Timing) and speak through
  SpokenCues.schedule, which now says a fingerboard's string and finger; chord names in Chords & words go through
  schedule(saying:lead:). SpokenCueTiming deleted.
- BandControls says what headphones add (full band over the speaker's drums, or the band over the click) and shows
  nothing where the speaker plays the full band. SongOpener and Songs previews no longer require headphones; Songs
  samples use filledBars with SongPlayback.band(notes:harmony:).
- ChartSongView hands the full-song Chords & words take to practice.
- Songs: learn.course through Preferences, XP hidden in Calm mode (also the song sheet's XP pill).
- Spaced review keeps a strum's muted and accented beats (stored only when present) and asks them again.
- ChordsView: the comment lists the qualities the recognizer still can't check.
- ShowMe and LessonMicrophone read the demo pitch through TestSupport.
- Content: gtr-4-3 grades its accent on beat one; curriculum rebuilt.
- HighwayRunway draws a gem from a Gem value (5 parameters). RhythmStep and RhythmStepTitle move unchanged to
  RhythmStep.swift, HighwayRunway unchanged to HighwayRunway.swift, so neither source file passes 2200 lines.

## 293. `3fa41ca` Audio, practice and demo scenes: demo pitch survives interruptions, recordings into lesson analysers, welcome and wobble sounds, Calm practice hub, interval legend, Band parameter structs

*2026-10-25T14:51:40-03:00 · Claude*

AudioService
- An interruption or media services reset no longer swaps a running demonstration pitch for a silent microphone:
  snapshot() restores the microphone only when a tap was installed, and (DEBUG) restore() restarts the demo pitch
  that was running. The route/configuration rebuild already required tapInstalled.
- DEBUG: TIMBRE_DEMO_CLIP=<path or bundle file> plays an AudioClip on a loop into `frames` while the demo pitch runs,
  in AudioClip.tapFrames buffers at real-time pace on the analysis queue, gated by SpeechGate like the tap.
- AudioSessionOwner.interfaceSoundIsAmbient(): prepares the ambient session and says whether interface sounds play
  in it now.

SoundFX
- playWelcome(): the motif only in the ambient session (mixes with the user's audio, keeps to the Ring/Silent switch).
- playWobble(frequency:): the miss wobble rendered at the target's pitch (bell-pitch octave rule), cached per pitch;
  the .wobble cue shares the same voice.

Practice
- PitchSniperView: a miss wobbles at the round's note; demo seed via TestSupport.isOn(.demoPitch); amber -> adjust.
- PracticeView: tool locks from FreeTier.opens; Calm mode hides the level card/tile and quests (iPad: the next badge
  takes their place beside Today).
- EarTrainingView: Calm mode drops the XP results tile; IntervalLegend spells out the short interval names the
  staircase draws (veil candidates, a chord's notes), read aloud as full names; coral -> danger.

DemoScenes
- Scene and probe reads through TestSupport; learn.course through Preferences.
- lesson-dead-string: the chord step with the demo pitch silent, and strumWithSilentString(pitches:hear:) delivering
  one fixed PolyReading whose highest harmonic-clear string is silent.

Band
- drums and comp take a BarSetup (beat, push, bar number, players, rate): 6 parameters each.

## 299. `f87f052` Audio: a superseded microphone start no longer lets the session go under the next one; one quiet retry before the alert

*2026-10-25T15:55:07-03:00 · Claude*

Two tuner starts overlap at launch (TuneView's onAppear and its scene-phase change both call autoListen while the first
start is still awaiting its background activation). The first one, called off after its activation, ran shutdown(), and
release(.lessons) removed the one .lessons holder the newer start had just inserted. A second later the owner's grace
expired: setActive(false) and setCategory(.ambient) under the running microphone engine, and the restart that followed
failed with 'what' (2003329396).

- shutdown() keeps the session while a start is waiting on its activation (activationsPending); a start called off on
  the way lets it go itself, so the session is released exactly when nothing listens, plays or is about to.
- An engine only running out a fade stops before the session's buffer duration and sample rate are set.
- start() opens the microphone through openMicrophone(), and a transient failure goes once more 300 ms later on a fresh
  engine (MicStartRetry); a missing input, a refused recording permission, a higher-priority session and a second
  failure still reach the alert, with the same text.
- Debug builds log which step failed (session activation, input and tap, output attach, engine start, output restart)
  with the error and the session's state.
- MicStartRetryTests cover the retry policy.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
Claude-Session: https://claude.ai/code/session_019ufcyLVTAsS1wDMvGhgM3C

```

 ios/TimbreAppTests/MicStartRetryTests.swift |  39 +++++++++++++++++++++++++
 2 files changed, 192 insertions(+), 14 deletions(-)
```

## 302. `9c3d22f` Song take clock after the click starts, a still HUD in the store scene, the AX5 tuner's Settings reachable, launch-proof accessibility job

*2026-10-25T16:25:21-03:00 · Claude*

- SongPerform.startMelody starts the click (and so the audio output) before the take's clock: on the runner the
  output start held the main thread 9.4 s inside startScene, after session.begin, so Kite Weather's one-bar lead
  was gone and the first ten live notes after the replayed 69 were missed (combo 0). Real takes gain the same:
  the runway no longer runs ahead of a slow-starting click by more than heardStart corrects.
- HighwayHUD holds its combo and multiplier still in the song-perform scene (no pop, ring or flame animation), as
  the score already was: the pop on every note kept the counter mid-change in every picture pair.
- storePerformFrame waits for the take itself (the stage's Perform giving way to the take's Stop, read from
  pictures, no query) and takes its pictures 4.5 s into it, instead of 7 s after the lesson opened.
- TuneView: the side controls get two shorter fallbacks (uncaptioned column, then Strum beside listening) and the
  header sits above the stage, so at AX5 Strum no longer spills over Settings and takes its tap (a11y-matrix ax5).
  AccessibilityMatrixUITests fails by name and closes Strum to tune when it opens instead of Settings.
- LocalizationLaunchUITests: the Arabic octave test runs on the demo pitch and reads pictures until the readout is
  unlocked (grey octave), so it measures the octave placement only.
- accessibility job: a warm-up launch after boot, and one more try for tests that failed only because the app
  never launched (tools/ci/launch_retry.py); every other failure stands.

## 305. `d4baf6e` Development history: from the Android port to wave 19

*2026-10-25T16:56:31-03:00 · Claude*

docs/history/DEVELOPMENT-HISTORY.md records, in order, how Timbre for iOS got
from the port (PR #1, 328b7d3) to 0bbf273, for a team picking the project up:

- the owner's brief and how the work was run (orchestrator and teams, the
  agent rules, independent reviewers, the six-juror panel and its scoring,
  macOS work on GitHub Actions only, English-only strings from wave 9);
- a phase map and the review score trajectory, checked against the review
  files (6.2, 6.6, then 6.9 to 8.9 for the single reviewer; jury 7.7 and
  8.3), with per-area scores for rounds 7-14 and per-angle scores for the
  jury rounds, and why the score fell in rounds 5, 10 and 15;
- every round's main findings, blockers first, and what the following wave
  changed, grouped by team or work package with commit hashes;
- the major product decisions and their reasons: subscriptions with no ads
  and no account, the free tier step by step, pricing and the $10k model,
  public-domain songs, Originals and songbooks instead of licences, the 3D
  stage and the Blender pipeline, CC0 samples, Studio Noir and the design
  system, Calm mode, left-handed mode, accessibility, analytics,
  localization, the CI gate, the store pipeline and engineering hygiene;
- wave 19 in detail: the day-1 contracts, the ten packages in worktrees,
  the merge order, the four-group integration, the gate, audio and QA
  follow-ups, the round-16 blockers after the wave and what was left;
- what each round said was still missing for #1, and what only the owner
  can do;
- the state at 0bbf273, the juries' standing "what not to do" rules, every
  commit in order, the review file index and a glossary.

The review and brief files live outside the repository; this document is
their durable summary.

Store Connect record, agreements, EU trader status and the Small Business
Program; hosting for the privacy policy, support page and remote settings;
GitHub settings (gate required on main, the apple-delivery environment with
the owner as required reviewer, its four secrets and the four repository
variables); the subscription group and products with prices, Family
Sharing, Billing Grace Period and the _b/_c price-test arms; the first
TestFlight delivery with ship_min_coverage 95 (English only today, the only
set that passes the critical-strings gate); the device sheet, including the
microphone start, stall and haptic-bleed checks from the latest audio work;

branches, Apple Watch screenshots, listing text, App Privacy answers matching
what the build sends, Accessibility Nutrition Labels, age rating, export
compliance, availability, review notes and the Holiday Carols In-App Event;
submission, manual release, and post-launch monitoring, offers, Custom
Product Pages and the carol week's remote settings. Apple's current rules
are cited at the end.

release blockers separated from growth: accounts and keys, the language
decision and translation volumes, pricing, song licensing, recordings,

## 319. `a8470ed` Accessibility audit: real failures fixed, misreadings listed with their measured contrast, the paywall audited with its plans

*2026-10-25T17:16:21-03:00 · Claude*

- The play-along's up-next rail: its tiles fade toward the end (fill, edge, bar) but the fret and note name no
  longer do. Faded, the second string's blue fell to 2.98:1 by the fourth tile ("B") and the first string's sky to
  3.19:1 ("E"); every string's colour now reads at 5.5:1 or more on its tile. The arrow before the rail is named
  "Next" (an existing, translated key) instead of by its symbol's name.
- Metronome: Slower and Faster take the tap across their whole 44 pt square (contentShape), not only on the glyph.
- Learn: a unit's star and lesson tally is one element ("8 of 18 stars, 3 of 6 lessons") at least 44 pt square,
  its extra height empty below the words, so nothing on the card moves.
- AccessibilityAuditUITests loads and empties the scheme's StoreKit configuration as TimbreUITests does, so the
  paywall audit sees the plans rather than "unavailable". Each `known` entry now carries why it is let through: the
  contrast readings checked against the audit's own screenshots (text and background colours, WCAG ratio) and found
  passing, with the ratio: text on Liquid Glass or a gradient wash, redrawn every frame, under the dock or a rail's
  edge fade, or the tuner's status line at opacity 0 while the peg label carries the call. The stale "8 of 15 stars"
  hit-region entry is gone with its fix; a known issue's reason is printed in the report.

## 321. `6eb0d7f` Band renders at user-initiated priority; the preview notes when a band is still rendering

*2026-10-25T17:34:01-03:00 · Claude*

The App Preview's highway clip had no band: the app's own engine output (the soundtrack's
source, tapped at the main mixer) held only the click for the whole 114 s take (189 engine
runs, about one per click), with the route read as headphones, so the full band was due.
The request path is sound (setMetronome then playBand, bandRequest kept, kit-only only on
the speaker, onSpeaker true for play-alongs and songs); the band joins only once its
rendering lands, and that rendering ran on a utility queue, behind the style index, in a
Debug build on a saturated runner (its screen recording froze 2.8 s in the tuner scene).

- AudioService: the band queue is user-initiated, as the tone queue is: a started take
  waits on it, and utility work is the first a busy device puts off.
- AudioService.bandRenderPending (read-only), and PreviewRecorder's `band` event carries
  `rendering`, so the next preview shows whether a missing band was still rendering or was
  never asked for.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
Claude-Session: https://claude.ai/code/session_019ufcyLVTAsS1wDMvGhgM3C

```
 ios/Timbre/Audio/AudioService.swift |  7 ++++++-
 ios/Timbre/Root/DemoScenes.swift    | 18 +++++++++++-------
 2 files changed, 17 insertions(+), 8 deletions(-)
```
